(7.33)

\displaystyle\lambda\leq{}

\displaystyle\overline{C}\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}}\delta_{0}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}}\lambda_{0}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}

(7.34)

\displaystyle\lambda\leq{}

\displaystyle\overline{C}\delta_{0}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}(\theta+\epsilon)^{-1}}\lambda_{0}

(7.35)

First we choose
\delta<1
, and
\overline{\alpha},\gamma
sufficiently small, and then show that, for
a
sufficiently large there exists a
\lambda
satisfying all the above inequalities.

First of all, notice that, since
\delta_{0}=\delta\lambda_{0}^{2\beta(b-1)}\gg 1
if
\delta
is fixed and
a
is sufficiently large, then (
7.31
) implies (
7.30
), and (
7.32
) implies (
7.35
) independently of the choice of
\alpha>0
, since
\theta+\epsilon<\beta<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}<1+\alpha
.

Hence, we are left with showing that (
7.31
) is compatible with (
7.32
)-(
7.34
).

The compatibility of (
7.31
) and (
7.32
), independently of
\alpha>0
, is straightforward, since
\delta_{0}\gg 1
when
a
is sufficiently large.

Inequality (
7.33
) does not contradict (
7.31
) provided we choose
\overline{\alpha}
so small that
\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\overline{\alpha}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\overline{\alpha}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\overline{\alpha}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\overline{\alpha}$}}$}}>1
, and then
a
sufficiently large.

The compatibility of (
7.31
) with (
7.34
) rewrites as

\lambda_{0}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\overline{C}\delta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\overline{K}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\overline{C}\delta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\overline{K}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\overline{C}\delta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\overline{K}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\overline{C}\delta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\overline{K}$}}$}}\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}}\delta_{0}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}}

and, inserting the definitions of
\delta_{0},\Lambdaup,\lambda_{1}
, as

\lambda_{0}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\overline{C}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\overline{K}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\overline{C}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\overline{K}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\overline{C}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\overline{K}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\overline{C}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\overline{K}$}}$}}\delta^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}+1}\lambda_{0}^{(2b-1){\left(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\beta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}\right)}}.

Hence the above reduces to showing that

\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\beta(2b-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\beta(2b-1)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\beta(2b-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\beta(2b-1)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2(\theta+\epsilon)$}}$}},

which holds since
b>1
and
\theta+\epsilon<\beta
.

The proof is thus complete.

8
Localized gluing step

Definition 8.1
(name=Decomposing the time interval,label=thm:defi:Subintervs+).

Let
0\leq T_{1}<T_{2}\leq T
such that
T_{2}-T_{1}>4\tau_{q}
. We define sequences of intervals
\{I_{i}\},\{J_{i}\}
as follows. Let

t_{i}\coloneq i\tau_{q}\qquad I_{i}\coloneq{\left[t_{i}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q},t_{i}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}\right]}\cap[0,T],

and let

\underline{n}\coloneq\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\,}cl@{}}\min{\left\{i:t_{i}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}\geq T_{1}\right\}}&T_{1}>0\\
0&T_{1}=0\end{array}\right.\qquad\qquad\overline{n}\coloneq\max{\left\{i:t_{i}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}\leq T_{2}\right\}}.

Moreover, define

\displaystyle J_{i}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\left(t_{i}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q},t_{i}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}\right)}\cap[0,T]\qquad\underline{n}\leq i\leq\overline{n}

\displaystyle J_{\underline{n}-1}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\left[0,t_{\underline{n}}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}\right)}\qquad\qquad J_{\overline{n}+1}\coloneq{\left(t_{\overline{n}}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q},T\right]}.

These form a pairwise disjoint decomposition of
[0,T]
:

[0,T]=J_{\underline{n}-1}\cup I_{\underline{n}-1}\cup[J_{\underline{n}}\cup\dotso\cup J_{\overline{n}}]\cup I_{\overline{n}}\cup J_{\overline{n}+1},

and

t_{\underline{n}}<T_{1}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 5$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 5$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$5$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$5$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}<T_{2}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 5$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 5$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$5$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$5$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}<t_{\overline{n}}.

Moreover, if
T_{1}>0
,
\underline{n}\geq 1
, otherwise we have both that
\underline{n}=0
and that
J_{\underline{n}-1}\cup I_{\underline{n}-1}=\varnothing
.

Given a subsolution
(v_{q},p_{q},R_{q})
with
\rho_{q}\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\operatorname{tr}R_{q}
and
\varrho_{q}\coloneq\Lambdaup^{-1}\rho_{q}
, we will define:

(\rho_{q,i},\varrho_{q,i},\ell_{q,i})\coloneq{\left(\rho_{q}(t_{i}),\varrho_{q}(t_{i}),\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\zeta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\zeta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-3.5pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}$}}$}}\right)},

where
\alpha,\gamma
are the parameters of Section
4
. Using (
8.11
) and assuming
a\gg 1
is sufficiently large (as in (
4.13
), depending on
\alpha,\gamma,b
), we may ensure that

\lambda_{q+1}^{-1}\leq\ell_{q}\leq\ell_{q,i}\leq\lambda_{q}^{-1}.

Since we will always be working with
\theta<\beta
, recalling
\ell_{q}^{-1}\leq\lambda_{q+1}
, and assuming
\epsilon\leq\alpha
, we observe that

\tau_{q}\ell_{q,i}^{-2\theta-\epsilon}\leq\tau_{q}\ell_{q}^{-2\theta-\epsilon}\leq\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\Lambdaup^{-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}{\left(\zeta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}\right)}^{-1}\lambda_{q+1}^{2\theta}\leq\lambda_{q}^{\beta-1+2b\theta}<1,

for
b
sufficiently close to 1.

Proposition 8.1
(name=Gluing step,label=thm:propo:Gluing).

Let
b,\beta,\alpha,\gamma
and
(\delta_{q},\lambda_{q},\Lambdaup,\zeta_{q},\ell_{q},\tau_{q})
be as in Section
4
, with

\displaystyle\alpha b<{}

\displaystyle\beta\gamma

(8.9)

\displaystyle b^{2}(1+\gamma)<{}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2\beta$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2\beta$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\beta$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1-\beta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2\beta$}}$}},

(8.10)

Let
[T_{1},T_{2}]\!\subset\![0,T]
with
|T_{2}-T_{1}|>4\tau_{q}
. Let
(v_{q},p_{q},R_{q})
be a strong subsolution on
[0,T]
which on
[T_{1},T_{2}]
satisfies the estimates

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$3$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4$}}$}}\delta_{q+2}\leq{}

\displaystyle\rho_{q}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 7$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 7$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$7$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$7$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\delta_{q+1}

(8.11)

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{R}_{q}\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q}^{1+\gamma}

(8.12)

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{q}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}\leq{}

\displaystyle M\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}

(8.13)

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\leq{}

\displaystyle M{\left(1+\!\sum_{i=0}^{q}\delta_{i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{i}^{\theta+\epsilon}\right)}

(8.14)

\displaystyle{\left|\partial_{t}\rho_{q}\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\rho_{q}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}

(8.15)

with some constant
M>0
, where

\rho_{q}\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\operatorname{tr}R_{q}\qquad\qquad\qquad\varrho_{q}\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rho_{q}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Lambdaup$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\rho_{q}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Lambdaup$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\rho_{q}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\Lambdaup$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\rho_{q}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\Lambdaup$}}$}}.

Define
\rho_{q,i},\varrho_{q,i},\ell_{q,i}
as in (
8
).

Then, provided
a\gg 1
is sufficiently large, there exists
(\overline{v}_{q},\overline{p}_{q},\overline{R}_{q})
solution of (
3.1
) on
[0,T]
such that

(\overline{v}_{q},\overline{p}_{q},\overline{R}_{q})=(v_{q},p_{q},R_{q})\qquad\text{on }[0,T]\smallsetminus[T_{1},T_{2}],

and on
[T_{1},T_{2}]
the following estimates hold:

\displaystyle{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}

(8.17)

\displaystyle{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}

(8.18)

\displaystyle{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\leq{}

\displaystyle M{\left(1+\sum_{i=0}^{q+1}\delta_{i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{i}^{\theta+\epsilon}\right)}

(8.19)

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\right\|}_{0}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}

(8.20)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 7$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 8$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 7$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 8$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$7$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$8$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$7$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$8$}}$}}\rho_{q}\leq{}

\displaystyle\overline{\rho}_{q}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 9$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 8$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 9$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 8$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$9$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$8$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$9$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$8$}}$}}\rho_{q}

(8.21)

\displaystyle{\left|\partial_{t}\overline{\rho}_{q}\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\overline{\rho}_{q}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}

(8.22)

\displaystyle{\left\|\rho_{q}-\overline{\rho}_{q}\right\|}_{0}={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\big({\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim\Lambdaup\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{1+\gamma}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}.

(8.23)

Moreover, on
[t_{\underline{n}},t_{\overline{n}}]
the following additional estimates hold for
t\!\in\!I_{i-1}\cup J_{i}\cup I_{i}
:

\displaystyle{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(8.24)

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}

(8.25)

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+\overline{v}_{q}\cdot\nabla)\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}.

(8.26)

Regarding the support of the Reynolds stress, we have that

\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}(\cdot,t)\equiv 0\qquad\forall t\!\in\!\bigcup_{i=\underline{n}}^{\overline{n}}J_{i}.

In terms of energy, we have that

\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\big({\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}(x,t)+\operatorname{tr}\overline{R}_{q}(x,t)\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux=\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\big({\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}(x,t)+\operatorname{tr}R_{q}(x,t)\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux,

and the function

\mathcal{T}_{g}\coloneq\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\int\limits_{0\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mut}{\Big({\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}v_{q}\right|}^{2}\Big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mus\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux,

satisfies

{\left|\partial_{t}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right|}\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q+1}^{\theta+\epsilon},

and therefore

{\left|\mathcal{T}_{g}(t)\right|}\lesssim t\Lambdaup\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{1+\gamma}\zeta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-6\alpha}\leq T\Lambdaup\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{1+\gamma}\zeta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-6\alpha}.

Finally, if
2\alpha<\beta\gamma
, then

{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\lesssim\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle b$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle b$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$b$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$b$}}$}}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}})\alpha}.

The proof closely follows the gluing procedure of
[
13 (D-R-Sz)
, Section 6]
, which in turn draws heavily from
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Sections 3-4]
. Recall that the solution is left unchanged outside
[T_{1},T_{2}]
and the gluing only happens in that interval. More precisely, recalling the decomposition (
8
):

•

The gluing procedure is carried out in the interval

J_{\underline{n}}\cup\dotso\cup J_{\overline{n}}={\left(t_{\underline{n}}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q},t_{\overline{n}}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}\right)};

•

The subsolution is left unchanged in
J_{\underline{n}-1}\cup J_{\overline{n}+1}
;

•

The intervals
I_{\underline{n}-1}
and
I_{\overline{n}}
are used as cutoff regions between the glued and unglued subsolutions.

Recall also that, since the trace
\rho_{q}={}^{1}/_{3}\operatorname{tr}R_{q}
has different lower and upper bounds on
[T_{1},T_{2}]
(respectively of order
\delta_{q+2}
and
\delta_{q+1}
), mollification with different parameters
\ell_{q,i}
depending on
\rho_{q}(t_{i})
on intervals of size
\tau_{q}
around the points
t_{i}
is necessary.

We will also make use of the following estimates.

Lemma 8.1
(name=Material derivative estimates for subsolutions and potentials,label=thm:lemma:MatDerEstSubsPot).

Let
(v,p,R)
,
(v^{\prime},p^{\prime},R^{\prime})
be two solutions of (
3.1
), and let
z\coloneq(-\Deltaup)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}v,z^{\prime}\coloneq(-\Deltaup)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}v
. Then the following estimates hold for every
N\!\in\!\mathbb{N},\alpha\!\in\!(0,1)
:

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta})(v-v^{\prime})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|v-v^{\prime}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}({\left\|v\right\|}_{\alpha}+{\left\|v^{\prime}\right\|}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|v-v^{\prime}\right\|}_{\alpha}({\left\|v\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{\left\|v^{\prime}\right\|}_{N+\alpha})+{\left\|R-R^{\prime}\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}

(8.32)

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta})(z^{\prime}-z)\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|z^{\prime}-z\right\|}_{N+\alpha}{\left\|v\right\|}_{1+\alpha}+{\left\|z^{\prime}-z\right\|}_{\alpha}{\left\|v\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|R^{\prime}-R\right\|}_{N+\alpha}.

(8.33)

The proof of (
8.32
) can be found in the proof of
[
20 (DR)
, Proposition 5.3]
, whereas that of (
8.33
) can be found within the proof of
[
20 (DR)
, Proposition 5.4]
. Note that, since the proofs require the Schauder estimates of
(??)
, this result does not hold for
\alpha=0
.

Proof.
(
(??)
)

Step 1: Mollification

Let
\phi
be a standard mollification kernel in space and define

\displaystyle v_{\ell_{q,i}}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle v_{q}\ast\phi_{\ell_{q,i}}

\displaystyle p_{\ell_{q,i}}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle p_{q}\ast\phi_{\ell_{q,i}}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}(|v_{q}|^{2}\ast\phi_{\ell}-|v_{\ell_{q,i}}|^{2})

\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{R}_{\ell_{q,i}}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{R}_{q}\ast\phi_{\ell_{q,i}}+(v_{q}\mathbin{\mathaccent 23{\otimes}}v_{q})\ast\phi_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\mathbin{\mathaccent 23{\otimes}}v_{\ell_{q,i}}.

With this definition, (
3.1
) holds for the triple
(v_{\ell_{q,i}},p_{\ell_{q,i}}.R_{\ell_{q,i}})
. Using the estimates (
8.12
) and (
8.13
), together with (
2.1
), we deduce that

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}=\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}

(8.34)

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(8.35)

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}

(8.36)

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{R}_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-\alpha}+\delta_{q}\lambda_{q}^{2+2\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{2-N-\alpha}

(8.37)

\displaystyle\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-\alpha}+\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}\lambda_{q}^{2+2\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{2}=\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.

(8.38)

To obtain (
8.38
), we also use the trivial identity
\int f\ast\phi_{\ell}=\int f
for
f=|v_{q}|^{2}
.

Step 2: Gluing procedure

Let
\{I_{i}\}_{\underline{n}\leq i\leq\overline{n}}
be the sequence of intervals corresponding to
[T_{1},T_{2}]
according to
(??)
above. We now fix a partition of unity on
[0,T]

\sum_{\mathclap{i=\underline{n}-1}}^{\overline{n}+1}\chi_{i}\equiv 1

subordinate to the decomposition (
8
), i.e.
[0,T]=J_{\underline{n}-1}\cup I_{\underline{n}-1}\cup[J_{\underline{n}}\cup\dotso\cup J_{\overline{n}}]\cup I_{\overline{n}}\cup J_{\overline{n}+1}
. More precisely, for each
\underline{n}-1\leq i\leq\overline{n}+1
, the function
\chi_{i}\geq 0
satisfies

\operatorname{supp}\chi_{i}\!\subset\!I_{i-1}\cup J_{i}\cup I_{i},\qquad\qquad\chi_{i}{\Big|\Big.}_{J_{i}}\equiv 1\qquad\qquad{\left|\partial_{t}^{N}\chi_{i}\right|}\lesssim\tau_{q}^{-N}\quad\forall N\geq 0.

We define

\overline{v}_{q}\coloneq\hskip-2.0pt\sum_{\mathclap{i=\underline{n}-1}}^{\overline{n}+1}\chi_{i}v_{i},\qquad\qquad\overline{p}_{q}^{(1)}\coloneq\hskip-2.0pt\sum_{\mathclap{i=\underline{n}-1}}^{\overline{n}+1}\chi_{i}p_{i},\mskip-20.0mu

where
(v_{i},p_{i})
is defined as follows. For
\underline{n}\leq i\leq\overline{n}
we define
(v_{i},p_{i})
as the solution of

\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}\partial_{t}v_{i}+\operatorname{div}(v_{i}\otimes v_{i})+\nabla p_{i}+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta}v_{i}=0\\
\operatorname{div}v_{i}=0\\
v_{i}(\cdot,t_{i})=v_{\ell_{q,i}}(\cdot,t_{i})\end{array}\right.,

and set
(v_{i},p_{i})=(v_{q},p_{q})
for
i=\underline{n}-1
and
i=\overline{n}+1
. Thus, we note first of all that
\operatorname{div}\overline{v}_{q}=0
, and moreover

(\overline{v}_{q},\overline{p}_{q}^{(1)})=(v_{q},p_{q}),\qquad t\!\in\![0,T]\smallsetminus[T_{1},T_{2}].

Next, we define
\overline{R}_{q}
. We have that
\chi_{i}+\chi_{i+1}=1
for
t\!\in\!J_{i}\cup I_{i}\cup J_{i+1}
, and therefore

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\overline{v}_{q}+\operatorname{div}(\overline{v}_{q}\otimes\overline{v}_{q})+\nabla\overline{p}_{q}^{(1)}+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta}\overline{v}_{q}={}

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\chi_{i}\cdot(v_{i}-v_{i+1})

\displaystyle{}-\chi_{i}\cdot(1-\chi_{i})\operatorname{div}(\!(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\otimes(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\!)

\displaystyle{}-\operatorname{div}(\chi_{i}R_{i}+(1-\chi_{i})R_{i+1})

for all
\underline{n}-1\leq i\leq\overline{n}
, where we wrote
R_{i}=0
for
\underline{n}\leq i\leq\overline{n}
and
R_{i}=R_{q}
otherwise. Thus, recalling the operator
\mathcal{R}
from
(??)
, set

\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}^{(1)}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\,}cl@{}}\begin{array}[]{c}\mskip-160.0mu-\partial_{t}\chi_{i}\mathcal{R}(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\\
{\mskip 16.0mu}+\chi_{i}\cdot(1-\chi_{i})(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\mathbin{\mathaccent 23{\otimes}}(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\end{array}&t\!\in\!I_{i}\\
0&t\!\in\!J_{i}\cup J_{i+1}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}^{(2)}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathclap{i=\underline{n}-1}}^{\overline{n}+1}\chi_{i}\mathaccent 23{R}_{i}=(\chi_{\underline{n}-1}+\chi_{\overline{n}+1})\mathaccent 23{R}_{q},

and

\overline{p}_{q}^{(2)}\coloneq\sum_{\mathclap{i=\underline{n}-1}}^{\overline{n}+1}\chi_{i}\cdot(1-\chi_{i}){\left({\left|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right|}^{2}-\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}{\left|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right)}.

Finally, we define

\overline{R}_{q}=\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}^{(1)}+\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}^{(2)}+\overline{\rho}_{q}\operatorname{Id},\qquad\overline{p}_{q}\coloneq\overline{p}_{q}^{(1)}+\overline{p}_{q}^{(2)},

where

\overline{\rho}_{q}\coloneq\rho_{q}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}{\big({\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux.

Define also

\mathcal{T}_{g}(t)\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\mathop{}\mkern-2.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.27777pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.56596pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.27084pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-2.0mu\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}{\Big({\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}v_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}\Big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux.

By construction, we have that

\partial_{t}\overline{v}_{q}+\operatorname{div}(\overline{v}_{q}\otimes\overline{v}_{q})+\nabla\overline{p}_{q}=-\operatorname{div}\overline{R}_{q},

and (
8.1
) and (
8.1
) hold. Moreover

\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}=0\qquad\forall t\!\in\!\bigcup_{i=\underline{n}}^{\overline{n}}J_{i}.

Step 3: Stability estimates on classical solutions

Throughout this step and the next, we will assume estimate (
8.21
), which will be proved in Step 5 below. This estimate will allow us to replace
\overline{\rho}_{q}
with
\rho_{q}
and viceversa whenever we need to do so in our estimates, since the two are of the same order.

Let us consider for the moment
\underline{n}\leq i\leq\overline{n}
. We recall the classical existence result for solutions of (
8
) found in
[
20 (DR)
, Proposition 3.5]
, by which
(v_{i},p_{i})
in (
8
) above is defined at least on an interval of length
\sim\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\|_{1+\alpha}^{-1}
. By (
8.35
) and (
4.11
), we have that

{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}\lesssim\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}=\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{4\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{\alpha}\leq\tau_{q}^{-1}.

Therefore, provided
a\gg 1
is sufficiently large,
v_{i}
is defined on
I_{i-1}\cup J_{i}\cup I_{i}
, so that
\overline{v}_{q}
in (
8
) is well-defined.

Next, we deduce from (
8.15
) that
|\partial_{t}\log\rho_{q}|\leq\delta_{q}^{{}^{1}\!/_{2}}\lambda_{q}=\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{4\alpha}
, so that, by assuming
a\gg 1
is sufficiently large, we may ensure that

\rho_{q}(t_{1})\leq 4\rho_{q}(t_{2})\qquad\forall t_{1},t_{2}\!\in\!I_{i-1}\cup J_{i}\cup I_{i},

for any
i
. In particular
\rho_{q}\sim\rho_{q,i}
and
\varrho_{q}\sim\varrho_{q,i}
in
I_{i-1}\cup J_{i}\cup I_{i}
. We apply
(??)
to
(v_{\ell_{q,i}},p_{\ell_{q,i}},R_{\ell_{q,i}})
and
(v_{i},p_{i},0)
, which, using (
8.34
), (
8.35
), (
8.37
), and (
8
), immediately yields

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta})(v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}({\left\|v_{i}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}+{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{1+\alpha})

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\alpha}({\left\|v_{i}\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}+{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha})

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|\mathaccent 23{R}_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\alpha}+\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-1-N-\alpha}.

(8.46)

Combining this with
(??)
, working first for the case
N=0
, then for
N=1
, and finally for the general case, yields

{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\int\limits_{t_{i}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mut}\tau_{q}^{-1}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mus+\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-1-\alpha}.

The Grönwall inequality then implies that

{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-1-\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}.

The case
N=0
of (
8
), together with (
8.34
), leads to

{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\leq\sum_{\mathclap{j=i-1}}^{i+1}({\left\|v_{j}-v_{\ell_{q,j}}\right\|}_{\alpha}+{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,j}}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha})\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}.

By (
8.21
), this is equivalent to (
8.17
).

The case
N=1
of (
8
) leads to

{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}=\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\zeta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}=\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}.

Combining the above estimate with (
8.13
) outside the gluing region and in
I_{\underline{n}-1}\cup I_{\overline{n}}
, and with (
8.35
) in
J_{\underline{n}}\cup I_{\underline{n}}\cup\dotso\cup I_{\overline{n}-1}\cup J_{\overline{n}}
, we deduce that (
8.18
) is verified. More generally, as we did above for
N=0
, we deduce from (
8.35
) and (
8
) that

{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}\right\|}_{1+N+\alpha}\leq\sum_{j=i-1}^{i+1}\chi_{j}{\left({\left\|v_{j}-v_{\ell_{q,j}}\right\|}_{1+N+\alpha}+{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,j}}\right\|}_{1+N+\alpha}\right)}\lesssim\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\qquad\qquad\forall t\!\in\!\operatorname{supp}\chi_{i}.

We have used the fact that
\ell_{q,i}\sim\ell_{q,i+1}\sim\ell_{q,i-1}
. The above inequality coincides with (
8.24
).

We also remark the following simple interpolation of the
N=0
and
N=1
cases of (
8
), which will be used in Step 5 below.

{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\alpha}^{1-\theta-\epsilon}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}^{\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-\theta-\epsilon-1-\alpha}.

Further in the proof, we will need estimates for
\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\|_{N+\alpha}
and
\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\|_{N+\alpha}
. Concerning the former, by applying the triangle inequality, we see that

{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\leq{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}.

The first and third term are estimated by (
8
). The second one is readily shown to satisfy the same estimate, so that

{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}.

As for the material derivative, we note that

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\cdot\nabla(v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}-v_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}-v_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}\eqcolon{}

\displaystyle I+II+III+IV.

We start by estimating
I
. Inserting (
8
) into (
8.46
) gives us

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta})(v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-1-N-\alpha}.

Using (
8
), we can easily conclude, by interpolating in (
8
), that

\displaystyle{\left\|(-\Deltaup)^{\theta}(v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha+2\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}^{1-2\theta-\epsilon}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha+1}^{2\theta+\epsilon}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\ell_{q,i}^{-2\theta-\epsilon}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-1-\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-1-N-\alpha},

so that

I\leq{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta})(v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{\left\|(-\Deltaup)^{\theta}(v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-1-N-\alpha}.

The term
IV
is handled similarly. Since
v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}
obeys the bound (
8
), using
(??)
we conclude that
II
also obeys the above bound. We now consider
III
, which can be estimated as follows:

III={\left\|(v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\cdot\nabla(v_{i+1}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{\alpha}\cdot\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{\alpha}\cdot\ell_{q,i}^{-N-1}=\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{2\alpha-N-1},

in particular satisfying the bound (
8
). We thus conclude that

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-1-N-\alpha}=\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-\alpha}.

Step 4: Estimates on the new Reynolds stress

As is done in
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Section 3.3]
, we define the vector potentials

z_{i}\coloneq(-\Deltaup)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}v_{i}\qquad z_{\ell_{q,i}}\coloneq(-\Deltaup)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}v_{\ell_{q,i}},\qquad z_{q}\coloneq(-\Deltaup)^{-1}\operatorname{curl}v_{q}

and, by a combination of
(??)
,
(??)
, and the Grönwall inequality similar to the one used to obtain (
8
) in Step 3 above, obtain that

{\left\|z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}.

Combining this with
(??)
yields

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta})(z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}.

By (
8
) and the Schauder estimates of
(??)
, we deduce that

\displaystyle{\left\|(-\Deltaup)^{\theta}(z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha+2\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha-1+2\theta+\epsilon}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-2\theta-\epsilon}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}.

(8.55)

By the triangle inequality, (
8.55
), and (
8
), we thus conclude that

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}.

Both (
8
) and (
8
) are valid in
I_{i-1}\cup J_{i}\cup I_{i}
for any
\underline{n}\leq i\leq\overline{n}
.

The sequel of this proof will require estimates on
z_{i}-z_{i+1}
, which means we must now bound
z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{\ell_{q,i+1}}
. We note that
z_{\ell_{q,i}}=z_{q}\ast\phi_{\ell_{q,i}}
and
z_{\ell_{q,i+1}}=z_{q}\ast\phi_{\ell_{q,i+1}}
, so that, using (
2.1
) and Schauder estimates (
(??)
), we get

{\left\|z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{\left\|z_{q}\right\|}_{2+\alpha}(\ell_{q,i}^{2-N}+\ell_{q,i+1}^{2-N})\lesssim\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}.

The final estimate for
z_{i}-z_{i+1}
is thus

{\left\|z_{i}-z_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\leq{\left\|z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{\left\|z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{\left\|z_{\ell_{q,i+1}}-z_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha},

slightly coarser than (
8
) above. As for the material derivatives, we must estimate

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(z_{i}-z_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\cdot\nabla(z_{\ell_{q,i+1}}-z_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\cdot\nabla)(z_{\ell_{q,i+1}}-z_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}\eqcolon{}

\displaystyle I+II+III+IV.

The terms I and IV are estimated by (
8
). To estimate
II
, we apply
(??)
to
(v_{\ell_{q,i}},p_{\ell_{q,i}},R_{\ell_{q,i}})
and
(v_{\ell_{q,i+1}},p_{\ell_{q,i+1}},R_{\ell_{q,i+1}})
and use (
8.35
) and (
8
), obtaining that

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta})(z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\rho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-\alpha}(\ell_{q,i}^{\alpha}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}+1).

We then note that, by interpolation

{\left\|(-\Deltaup)^{\theta}(z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-2\theta-\epsilon}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}.

The above two bounds combine to yield

II\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}.

Coming to
III
, we estimate it by combining (
8
) with (
8
):

\displaystyle{\left\|(v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\cdot\nabla(z_{i+1}-z_{\ell_{q,i+1}})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{\alpha}\cdot\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}\cdot\ell_{q,i}^{-N-1}

\displaystyle{}\leq{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{\alpha-N}.

(8.60)

Combining (
8
), (
8
), and (
8.60
), we thus obtain that

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(z_{i}-z_{I+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}.

Recalling the expression for
\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}
in (
8
), using (
8
), (
8
), and (
8
), we obtain, as in the proof of
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Proposition 4.3]
, that

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\tau_{q}^{-1}{\left\|z_{i}-z_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}+\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N+\alpha}\cdot\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\varrho_{q,i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}(\ell_{q}^{-\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha}+\ell_{q,i}^{3\alpha}).

This, together with (
8.21
), gives us (
8.25
).

As for (
8.26
), we note that

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+\overline{v}_{q}\cdot\nabla)\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\tau_{q}^{-2}{\left\|z_{i}-z_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+\tau_{q}^{-1}{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(z_{i}-z_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+{}

\displaystyle{}+\tau_{q}^{-1}{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}{\left\|z_{i}-z_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}+\tau_{q}^{-1}{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}{\left\|z_{i}-z_{i+1}\right\|}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+\tau_{q}^{-1}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\right\|}_{N+\alpha}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\cdot\nabla)(v_{i}-v_{i+1})\right\|}_{\alpha}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}

\displaystyle{}+{\left\|(v_{\ell_{q,i}}-\overline{v}_{q})\cdot\nabla\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}.

Combining the above bound on
\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}
with (
8
), (
8
), (
8.35
), (
8
), (
8
), and the bound (
8
) applied to
v_{\ell_{q,i}}-\overline{v}_{q}
, we obtain that

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+\overline{v}_{q}\cdot\nabla)\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup\tau_{q}^{-1}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha},

which yields (
8.26
) once combined with (
8.21
).

Step 5:
\overline{\rho}_{q}
,
\mathcal{T}_{g}
, and (
8.1
)

Next, we estimate
\overline{\rho}_{q}
, recalling its definition in (
8
). We wish to estimate
\overline{\rho}_{q}-\rho_{q}
. We note that

{\left\|\overline{\rho}_{q}-\rho_{q}\right\|}_{0}=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\leq{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}+{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}.

The second term above is already estimated by (
8.38
), so we proceed to estimate the first term.

As in
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Proposition 4.4]
, one has that

\displaystyle{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}={}

\displaystyle\chi_{i}(|v_{i}|^{2}-|v_{\ell_{q,i}}|^{2})+(1-\chi_{i})(|v_{i+1}|^{2}-|v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}|^{2})

\displaystyle{}+(1-\chi_{i})(|v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}|^{2}-|v_{\ell_{q,i}}|^{2})-\chi_{i}(1-\chi_{i})|v_{i}-v_{i+1}|^{2}.

Therefore

\displaystyle{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{i}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}+{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{i+1}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}

\displaystyle{}+{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}+{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}.

(8.63)

We start by estimating the fourth term as follows by using (
8
):

{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\leq{\left\|v_{i}-v_{i+1}\right\|}_{\alpha}^{2}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.

We then proceed to estimate the third term in (
8.63
) by using the triangle inequality, (
8.38
), and the fact
\varrho_{q,i}\sim\varrho_{q,i+1}
:

\displaystyle{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\big({\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\right|}^{2}\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\big({\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}+{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\big({\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i+1}}\right|}^{2}\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.\

(8.65)

The first and second terms in (
8.63
) are estimated in similar ways, so we only estimate the former. To that end, we proceed in a way similar to
[
20 (DR)
, Proposition 5.5]
. We start by using the fact that
(v_{i},p_{i},0)
and
(v_{\ell_{q,i}},p_{\ell_{q,i}},\mathaccent 23{R}_{\ell_{q,i}})
are subsolutions,
(??)
, (
8.35
) and (
8.37
), and (
8.36
) and (
8
), to obtain that

\displaystyle{\left|\dfrac{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mu}}{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mut}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{i}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\leq{}

\displaystyle 2{\left({\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\mathfrak{D}v_{\ell_{q,i}}\mathbin{:}\mathaccent 23{R}_{\ell_{q,i}}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}+{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}v_{i}\right|}^{2}-{\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\right)}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}{\left\|\mathaccent 23{R}_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\alpha}+{\left\|v_{i}+v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}{\left\|v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\cdot\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-\alpha}+\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\cdot\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}\ell_{q,i}^{-\theta-\epsilon-1-\alpha}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}(F_{1}+F_{2}),

where we write

\displaystyle F_{1}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-\alpha}\tau_{q}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}

\displaystyle F_{2}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\tau_{q}^{2}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-1-\theta}\ell_{q,i}^{-\epsilon-\alpha}.

It is easy to see that

F_{1}=\ell_{q}^{4\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-\alpha}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lesssim 1.
