b^{2}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}(1-\beta)}=\lambda_{q}^{-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}(1-\beta-2b^{2}\beta)}\leq 1.

We have thus proved that

F_{2}\lesssim 1,

provided
\alpha,\gamma,\epsilon\ll 1
are sufficiently small and
a
is sufficiently large. This means that

{\left|\dfrac{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mu}}{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mut}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{i}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.

Combining (
8.63
)-(
8
), we conclude that

{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.

Estimates (
8
) and (
8.38
) imply

{\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.

This proves in particular that
\overline{\rho}_{q}\sim\rho_{q}
and (
8.21
), as well as (
8.23
).

Similarly, using (
3.1
) for
(v_{q},p_{q},R_{q})
and
(v_{\ell_{q,i}},p_{\ell_{q,i}},\mathaccent 23{R}_{\ell_{q,i}})
first, and for
(\overline{v}_{q},\overline{p}_{q},\overline{R}_{q})
and
(v_{\ell_{q,i}},p_{\ell_{q,i}},R_{\ell_{q,i}})
afterwards, we also deduce that

\displaystyle{\left|\dfrac{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mu}}{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mut}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{q}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}

(8.68)

\displaystyle{\left|\dfrac{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mu}}{{\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mut}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|v_{\ell_{q,i}}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.

(8.69)

Combining (
8.68
) and (
8.69
), we get

{\left|\partial_{t}\overline{\rho}_{q}-\partial_{t}\rho_{q}\right|}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}.

To prove (
8.22
) note that

\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{2\alpha}\lesssim\rho_{q}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}\cdot\lambda_{q+1}^{2\alpha-2\beta\gamma}\lesssim\rho_{q}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q},

where we used the definitions of
\tau_{q},\zeta_{q+1}
, (
8.11
), the relations
\ell_{q}^{-1}\leq\lambda_{q+1}
and
\Lambdaup\varrho_{q,i}=\rho_{q,i}\sim\rho_{q}
, and the fact that
\alpha<\alpha b<\beta\gamma
, which follows from (
8.9
) since
b>1
. Therefore, since we showed above that
\overline{\rho}_{q}\sim\rho_{q}
, we have (
8.22
).

It remains to estimate
{\big\|\mathaccent 23{\overline{R}}_{q}\big\|}_{0}
on
[T_{1},T_{2}]
in order to verify (
8.20
) for the Reynolds stress. We already obtained (
8.25
) on
J_{\underline{n}}\cup\dotso\cup J_{\overline{n}}
(recall the decomposition (
8
)). Moreover, on
J_{\underline{n}-1}\cup J_{\overline{n}+1}
the subsolution remains unchanged, so there is nothing to prove. We are then left with the task of proving (
8.20
) on the cut-off regions
I_{\underline{n}-1}
and
I_{\overline{n}}
.

To do so, we need to estimate
\|v_{i}-v_{q}\|_{\alpha}
and
\|z_{i}-z_{q}\|_{\alpha}
. For the former, we combine estimates of
v_{i}-v_{\ell_{q,i}}
and of
v_{\ell_{q,i}}-v_{q}
. For the latter, we only need to estimate
\|z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{q}\|_{\alpha}
, since we already handled
z_{i}-z_{\ell_{q,i}}
above. One has that, by (
2.1
),
(??)
(Schauder estimates), and (
8.13
)

{\left\|z_{\ell_{q,i}}-z_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\lesssim{\left\|z_{q}\right\|}_{2+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{2}\lesssim{\left\|\operatorname{curl}v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{2}\lesssim\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{2}=\Lambdaup\tau_{q}\varrho_{q,i}^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{-\alpha},

which gives us (
8.20
) as desired.

We then have to verify (
8.19
) and (
8.1
). To that end, we observe that

{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\leq{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}+{\left\|v_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}.

The second term is estimated by (
8.14
). As for the first one, we note that

{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\overline{\varrho}_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1+\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}\lesssim\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\zeta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha}\leq\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha},

where the first step is due to (
8
). We can thus estimate
\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\|_{\theta+\epsilon}
by interpolation:

\displaystyle{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}^{1-\theta-\epsilon}{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{1+\alpha}^{\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim(\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha})^{1-\theta-\epsilon}\cdot{\left(\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\right)}^{\theta+\epsilon}

\displaystyle{}\leq{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q+1}^{\theta+\epsilon}\cdot\ell_{q}^{\alpha(1-2\theta-2\epsilon)}.

Lastly

{\left|\partial_{t}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right|}={\left|\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt{\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\overline{v}_{q}\right|}^{2}-{\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}v_{q}\right|}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\right|}\lesssim{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}+v_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\lesssim\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q+1}^{\theta+\epsilon},

thus proving (
8.1
).

We conclude the proof by obtaining (
8.1
). To that end, we first note that (
8.21
) and (
8.11
) combine to give us
\overline{\varrho}_{q}\lesssim\zeta_{q+1}
. Combining this with (
8.17
), we conclude that

{\left\|\overline{v}_{q}-v_{q}\right\|}_{\alpha}\lesssim\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\zeta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q}^{\alpha},

thus reducing (
8.1
) to

\lambda_{q+1}^{-\beta\gamma}\ell_{q}^{(\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle b$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle b$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$b$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$b$}}$}})\alpha}\lesssim 1.

Since
\ell_{q}^{-1}\leq\lambda_{q+1}
, the above follows from

-\beta\gamma+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle b$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle b$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$b$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$b$}}$}}\alpha-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\alpha<0\iff\alpha<\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2b\beta\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4-b$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2b\beta\gamma$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4-b$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2b\beta\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4-b$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2b\beta\gamma$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4-b$}}$}}.

We recall that we wish to obtain (
8.1
) only under the assumption
2\alpha<\beta\gamma
. This means the above relation follows from

\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2b$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 4-b$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2b$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 4-b$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2b$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4-b$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2b$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$4-b$}}$}}>\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\iff 5b>4,

which in turn follows from
b>1
. The proof is thus complete.

Remark 8.1
(name=Multi-gluing,label=thm:oss:MultiGluing).

(??)
can easily be extended to a pairwise disjoint union of intervals
[T_{1}^{(i)},T_{2}^{(i)}]\!\subset\![0,T]
with
T_{2}^{(i)}-T_{1}^{(i)}\geq 4\tau_{q}
and
T_{2}^{(i)}<T_{1}^{(i+1)}
.

9
Perturbation step

Proposition 9.1
(name=Main Perturbation Step,label=thm:propo:MPS).

Let
b,\beta,\alpha,\gamma,(\delta_{q},\lambda_{q},\Lambdaup,\zeta_{q},\ell_{q},\tau_{q})
be as in Section
4
with

\displaystyle\alpha<{}

\displaystyle\beta\gamma.

(9.1)

Let
[T_{1},T_{2}]\!\subset\![0,T]
and let
t_{i},I_{i},J_{i}
be as in (
8.1
). Let
(v,p,R)
be a smooth strong subsolution on
[T_{1},T_{2}]
satisfying

\displaystyle{\left\|R\right\|}_{1}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varrho^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-1}

(9.2)

\displaystyle\delta_{q+2}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\rho\lesssim\delta_{q+1}

(9.3)

\displaystyle{\left\|v\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle C_{P}

(9.4)

on
K_{i}\coloneq[(i-1+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}})\tau_{q},(i+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$2$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}})\tau_{q}]
for
\underline{n}-1\leq i\leq\overline{n}+1
, where the
\ell_{q,i}
are defined as in (
8
), and
C_{P}
is a geometric constant. Further, let
\psi:[0,T]\to[0,1]
be a cutoff function and
S\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{3}\times[T_{1},T_{2}];\mathcal{S}^{3\times 3})
be a smooth matrix field with

S(x,t)=\sigma(t)\operatorname{Id}+\mathaccent 23{S}(x,t)=\Lambdaup\varsigma(t)\operatorname{Id}+\mathaccent 23{S}(x,t),

where
\mathaccent 23{S}={}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}
is traceless,
\sigma={}^{2}\sigma
, and
(\varsigma,\varsigma)\coloneq\Lambdaup^{-1}(\sigma,\sigma)
. Suppose
\psi
satisfies

\displaystyle{\left|{}^{\prime}\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q},

(9.6)

and
\sigma
satisfies

\displaystyle 0\leq{}

\displaystyle\sigma(t)\leq 4\delta_{q+1}

(9.7)

\displaystyle\sigma|_{K_{i}}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\rho(t_{i})

(9.8)

\displaystyle{\left|\partial_{t}\sigma\right|}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\sigma\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q},

(9.9)

Moreover, assume that for any
N\geq 0,t\!\in\!I_{i-1}\cup J_{i}\cup I_{i},\underline{n}\leq i\leq\overline{n}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varsigma^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}

(9.10)

\displaystyle{\left\|v\right\|}_{N+1+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(9.11)

\displaystyle{\left\|v\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\leq{}

\displaystyle M{\left(1+\sum_{i=0}^{q+1}\Lambdaup^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{i}^{\theta+\epsilon-\beta}\right)}

(9.12)

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla)\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\Lambdaup\varsigma^{1+\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-6\alpha}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q},

(9.13)

Finally, assume that

\operatorname{supp}\mathaccent 23{S}\!\subseteq\!\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\bigcup_{i}I_{i}.

Then, provided
a\gg 1
is sufficiently large (depending on the implicit constants in (
9.9
), (
9.10
), (
9.11
), and (
9.13
)), there exist smooth
(\tilde{v},\tilde{p})\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{3}\times[T_{1},T_{2}];\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R})
and a smooth matrix field
\mathcal{E}\!\in\!\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{3}\times[T_{1},T_{2}];\mathcal{S}^{3\times 3})
with
\operatorname{supp}\mathcal{E}\!\subset\!\operatorname{supp}S
such that, setting
\tilde{R}_{1}\coloneq R-S-\mathcal{E}
, the triple
(\tilde{v},\tilde{p},\tilde{R})
is a strong subsolution with

\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt|\tilde{v}|^{2}+\operatorname{tr}\tilde{R}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux=\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt|v|^{2}+\operatorname{tr}R\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\qquad\forall t.

Moreover, we have the estimates

\displaystyle{\left\|\tilde{v}-v\right\|}_{H^{-1}}\leq

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle M$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\ell_{q,i}^{-1}\lambda_{q+1}^{-1}

(9.16)

\displaystyle{\left\|\tilde{v}-v\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle M$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}

(9.17)

\displaystyle{\left\|\tilde{v}-v\right\|}_{1+\alpha}\leq{}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle M$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$M$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q+1}^{1+\alpha}

(9.18)

\displaystyle{\left\|\tilde{v}-v\right\|}_{\theta+\epsilon}\leq{}

\displaystyle M\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q+1}^{\theta+\epsilon},

(9.19)

and the error
\mathcal{E}
satisfies the estimates

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathcal{E}\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q+2}\lambda_{q+1}^{-6\alpha}

(9.20)

\displaystyle{\left|\partial_{t}\operatorname{tr}\mathcal{E}\right|}\leq{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q+2}\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q+1}^{1-6\alpha}.

(9.21)

Finally, setting

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{p}(t)\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\int\limits_{0\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mut}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\Big({\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\tilde{v}\right|}^{2}-{\left|(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}v\right|}^{2}\Big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mus

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\int\limits_{0\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mut}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\Big((-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}(\tilde{v}+v)\cdot(-\Deltaup)^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\theta$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\theta$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}(\tilde{v}-v)\Big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mus,

we have that

{\left|\partial_{t}\mathcal{T}_{p}\right|}\lesssim\Lambdaup\lambda_{q}^{\theta+\epsilon-\beta},

for any
\epsilon>0
. Thus,
\mathcal{T}_{p}\operatorname{Id}
satisfies (
9.21
) for all
t\!\in\![0,T]
, and (
9.20
) only for small times.

The proof extends
[
13 (D-R-Sz)
, Section 7]
, which is a localization of the argument carried out in
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Section 5]
. The difference between
[
13 (D-R-Sz)
]
and
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
]
is that the latter absorbs the whole
R
with the perturbation flow, whereas the former, as well as the proof below, aims to only absorb
S
.

Proof.

Step 1: Squiggling Stripes and the Stress Tensors
\tilde{S}_{i}

As in
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Lemma 5.3]
, we choose a family of smooth non-negative
\eta_{i}=\eta_{i}(x,t)
with the following properties:

\displaystyle\eta_{i}\!\in\!{}

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{3}\times[T_{1},T_{2}];[0,1])

(9.23)

\displaystyle\operatorname{supp}\eta_{i}\cap\operatorname{supp}\eta_{j}={}

\displaystyle\varnothing\qquad\qquad\forall i\neq j

(9.24)

\displaystyle\mathbb{T}^{3}\times I_{i}\!\subset\!{}

\displaystyle{\left\{(x.t):\eta_{i}(x,t)=1\right\}}

(9.25)

\displaystyle\operatorname{supp}\eta_{i}\!\subseteq\!{}

\displaystyle\mathbb{T}^{3}\times(J_{i}\cup I_{i}\cup J_{i+1})

(9.26)

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle\mathbb{T}^{3}\times{\left\{{\left(t_{i}-\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q},t_{i}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\tau_{q}\right)}\cap[0,T]\right\}}

\displaystyle\exists c_{0}>0:\quad\sum_{i}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\eta_{i}^{2}(x,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux\geq{}

\displaystyle c_{0}\quad\forall t\!\in\![0,T]

(9.27)

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{t}^{N}\eta_{i}\right\|}_{m}\leq{}

\displaystyle C(N,m)\tau_{q}^{-N}\qquad N,m\geq 0,

(9.28)

where the
c_{0}
in (
9.27
) is a geometric constant. Define

\sigma_{i}(x,t)\coloneq{\left|\mathbb{T}^{3}\right|}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\eta_{i}^{2}(x,t)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}(y,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\eta_{i}^{2}(x,t)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}(y,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\eta_{i}^{2}(x,t)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}(y,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\eta_{i}^{2}(x,t)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}(y,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5muy$}}$}}\sigma(t),

so that
\sum_{i}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\sigma_{i}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\sigma\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux
. Using the inverse flow
\Phiup_{i}
starting at time
t_{i}

\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla)\Phiup_{i}=0\\
\Phiup_{i}(x,t_{i})=x\end{array}\right.,

set

\displaystyle S_{i}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\sigma_{i}\operatorname{Id}+\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{i}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}S_{i}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}S_{i}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}S_{i}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}S_{i}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}=\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}{\left(\operatorname{Id}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\sum_{j}\int\eta_{j}^{2}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}$}}\mathaccent 23{S}\right)}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}.

One can check from (
9.27
)-(
9.28
) and from (
9.7
) and (
9.8
) that

\displaystyle{\left\|\sigma_{i}\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle 4|\mathbb{T}^{3}|c_{0}^{-1}\delta_{q+1}

(9.29)

\displaystyle{\left\|\sigma_{i}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\rho_{i}\coloneq\rho(t_{i})\lesssim\delta_{q+1},

(9.30)

and moreover, since by (
9.1
)
\operatorname{supp}\mathaccent 23{S}\!\subseteq\!{\left\{\sum_{i}\eta_{i}^{2}=1\right\}}

\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\operatorname{tr}\sum_{i}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0ptS_{i}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\operatorname{tr}S.\qquad\qquad\sum_{i}\mathaccent 23{S}_{i}=\mathaccent 23{S}

We next claim that for all
(x,t)

\tilde{S}_{i}(x,t)\!\in\!B_{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}(\operatorname{Id})\!\subset\!\mathcal{S}_{+}^{3\times 3},

where
B_{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}(\operatorname{Id})
is the ball of radius
\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}
centered at the identity
\operatorname{Id}
in
\mathcal{S}^{3\times 3}
. Indeed, by the classical estimates on transport equations reported in
(??)

{\left\|\nabla\Phiup_{i}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{0}\lesssim\tau_{q}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}=\ell_{q}^{4\alpha}\lambda_{q}^{\alpha}\leq\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}

for
t\!\in\!J_{i}\cup I_{i}\cup J_{i+1}
, since this is an interval of length
{\left|J_{i}\cup I_{i}\cup J_{i+1}\right|}\sim\tau_{q}
. Using (
9.7
), (
9.10
) and (
4.13
), we also have that, for any
N\geq 0

{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim\varsigma\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lesssim\zeta_{q+1}\lambda_{q+1}^{2\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}=\lambda_{q+1}^{2\alpha-2\beta\gamma}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}.

Then, using the decomposition

\tilde{S}_{i}-\operatorname{Id}=\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\eta_{i}^{2}\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}+\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}-\operatorname{Id})+\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}-\operatorname{Id},

we deduce from (
9
)-(
9
) that

{\left|\tilde{S}_{i}-\operatorname{Id}\right|}\lesssim(1+\ell_{q}^{3\alpha})\lambda_{q+1}^{2\alpha-2\beta\gamma}(1+\ell_{q}^{3\alpha})+2\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\leq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}},

provided
a\gg 1
is sufficiently large, since we assumed
\alpha<\beta\gamma
in (
9.1
). This verifies (
9
).

Step 2: The perturbation
w
.

Now we can define the perturbation term as

w_{o}\coloneq\sum_{i}\sqrt{\sigma_{i}}(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}W(\tilde{S}_{i},\lambda_{q+1}\Phiup_{i})=\sum_{i}w_{oi},

where
W
are the Mikado flows on the compact set
B_{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}(\operatorname{Id})
as defined in
(??)
. Notice that the supports of the
w_{oi}
are disjoint and, using the Fourier series representation of the Mikado flows

w_{oi}\coloneq\sum_{k\neq 0}(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}b_{i,k}A_{k}e^{i\lambda_{q+1}k\cdot\Phiup_{i}},

where we write

b_{i,k}(x,t)\coloneq\sqrt{\sigma_{i}(x,t)}a_{k}(\tilde{S}_{i}(x,t)\!).

We define
w_{c}
so that
w\coloneq w_{o}+w_{c}
is divergence-free:

w_{c}\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle i$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda_{q+1}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda_{q+1}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$i$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda_{q+1}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$i$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda_{q+1}$}}$}}\cdot\mskip-4.0mu\sum_{i,k\neq 0}\mathfrak{D}(b_{i,k})\times\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle|k|^{2}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle|k|^{2}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|k|^{2}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|k|^{2}$}}$}}e^{i\lambda_{q+1}k\cdot\Phiup_{i}}=\sum_{i,k\neq 0}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle c_{i,k}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\lambda_{q+1}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle c_{i,k}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\lambda_{q+1}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$c_{i,k}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda_{q+1}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$c_{i,k}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\lambda_{q+1}$}}$}}e^{i\lambda_{q+1}k\cdot\Phiup_{i}},

where we write

c_{i,k}=\mathfrak{D}(b_{i,k})\times\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle|k|^{2}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle|k|^{2}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|k|^{2}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{T}\cdot(k\times A_{k})$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|k|^{2}$}}$}}.

Define then

\displaystyle w\coloneq{}

\displaystyle w_{o}+w_{c}

\displaystyle\tilde{v}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle v+w

\displaystyle\tilde{p}\coloneq{}

\displaystyle p-\sum_{i}\sigma_{i}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(x,t)\coloneq{}

\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{\mathcal{E}}^{(1)}(x,t)+\mathcal{E}^{(2)},

where

\mathaccent 23{\mathcal{E}}^{(1)}\coloneq\mathcal{R}(\partial_{t}\tilde{v}+\operatorname{div}(\tilde{v}\otimes\tilde{v})+\nabla\tilde{p}+(-\Deltaup)^{\theta}\tilde{v}+\operatorname{div}(R-S)\!),

with
\mathcal{R}
being the anti-divergence operator defined in
(??)
, and

\mathcal{E}^{(2)}(t)\coloneq\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\operatorname{Id}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\operatorname{Id}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\operatorname{Id}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\operatorname{Id}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$3$}}$}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}{\big(|\tilde{v}|^{2}-|v|^{2}-\operatorname{tr}S\big)}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux.

Equations (
9.1
) and (
3.1
) follow by construction.

Step 3: Estimates on the perturbation

The estimates on
\tilde{v}
follow similarly to the ones for
v_{q+1}
in
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Section 5-6]
. Obtaining those requires estimates on the coeffcients
b_{i,k},c_{i,k}
, which in turn require estimates of
\tilde{S}_{i}
and estimates of
\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}
. The latter read as follows:

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{N}+{\left\|(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(9.39)

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}+{\left\|(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\ell_{q}^{3\alpha\bb{1}_{N\neq 0}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(9.40)

\displaystyle{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla)\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q}^{3\alpha\bb{1}_{N\neq 0}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}.

(9.41)

To obtain these, we first observe that
\Phiup_{i}
is a diffeomorphism, which implies both
\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}
and
(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}
are bounded, thus yielding the
N=0
case of (
9.40
). To obtain (
9.39
), we start by combining (
9
) with the
N=0
case of (
9.40
), thus obtaining that

{\left\|(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{0}\leq{\left\|(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\operatorname{Id}-\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}\lesssim\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}.

This yields (
9.39
) for
N=0
. For
N\geq 1
, we note that

{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{N}+{\left\|(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{0}+{\left\|(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}-\operatorname{Id}\right\|}_{0}+{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N-1}+{\left\|\mathfrak{D}(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}\right\|}_{N-1}.

The other cases of (
9.39
) follow by combining its
N=0
case with the
N\geq 1
cases of (
9.40
).

The estimates for
\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\|_{N}
for
N\geq 1
follow from
(??)
. By combining (
2.20
) with
(??)
, we obtain that

{\left\|(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla)\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}{\left\|\nabla v\right\|}_{0}+{\left\|\nabla v\right\|}_{N}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}.

Estimates (
9.40
) and (
9.11
) then yield (
9.41
).

To complete the proof of (
9.40
), we are left with estimating
\|(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i})^{-1}\|_{N}
. We note that
\mathfrak{D}^{N}(\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})=0
for
N\geq 1
. We then use the Leibniz rule and the chain rule to write

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}^{2}(\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})={}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}(\!(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})=(\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})^{2}+(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}^{3}(\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})={}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}(\mathfrak{D}^{2}(\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})\!)

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle(\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})^{3}+3(\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})+(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}^{4}(\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})={}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}(\mathfrak{D}^{3}(\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})\!)

\displaystyle{}={}

\displaystyle(\mathfrak{D}^{4}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})^{4}+6(\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})^{2}\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}+3(\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})^{2}

\displaystyle{}+4(\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})+(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})\mathfrak{D}^{4}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}.

From these, we can see that

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}^{2}\lesssim\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-1}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}{\left({\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}^{3}+3{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}\right)}\lesssim\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-2}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{4}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}\leq{}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}\left({\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{4}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}^{4}+6{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}^{2}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}\right.

\displaystyle{}+\left.3{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}^{2}+4{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{2}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}^{3}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}\right\|}_{0}\right)\lesssim\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\ell_{q,i}^{-3}.

These two examples show us that

\mathfrak{D}^{M}(\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})=(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})\mathfrak{D}^{M}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}+\text{other terms},

where the other terms are of the form
(\mathfrak{D}^{k}\Phiup_{i}\circ\Phiup_{i}^{-1})(\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})^{\ell}(\mathfrak{D}^{m}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})^{n}
, where
k-1+(m-1)n=M-1
and
m<M
. If we assume (
9.40
) for
N<M-1
, we see that such terms are estimated as
\ell_{q,i}^{-(k-1)}\ell_{q,i}^{-(m-1)n}=\ell_{q,i}^{-(1-M)}
, thus so is
\mathfrak{D}^{M}\Phiup_{i}^{-1}=\mathfrak{D}(\mathfrak{D}^{M-1}\Phiup_{i}^{-1})
, which proves (
9.40
) for
N=M-1
. Thus, by induction, the estimate (
9.40
) is proved.

The following estimates then follow precisely as in
[
4 (B-DL-Sz-V)
, Propositions 5.7 and 5.9]
:

\displaystyle{\left\|\tilde{S}_{i}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(9.42)

\displaystyle{\left\|b_{i,k}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\rho_{i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}|k|^{-6}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(9.43)

\displaystyle{\left\|c_{i,k}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\rho_{i}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}|k|^{-6}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-1}

(9.44)

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\tilde{S}_{i}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}

(9.45)

(9.46)

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}c_{i,k}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q+1}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q,i}^{-N-1}|k|^{-6}.

(9.47)

9.42
), observe first that, by its definition, we have that

\tilde{S}_{i}=\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}+\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sum_{j}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\eta_{j}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sum_{j}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\eta_{j}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\sum_{j}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\eta_{j}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\sum_{j}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\eta_{j}^{2}\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\sigma$}}$}}\mathaccent 23{S}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i},

and therefore

{\left\|\tilde{S}_{i}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}+{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}\right\|}_{0}+{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}\right\|}_{N},

where we used that
\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\|_{0}\lesssim 1
. By (
9.40
), the first term above obeys (
9.42
). To estimate the remaining two terms, we use (

) and (

) to obtain that

{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle{\left\|\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{${\left\|\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{${\left\|\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N+\alpha}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}\lesssim\varsigma\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-2\alpha}\lesssim\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\lambda_{q+1}^{2\alpha-2\beta\gamma}.

Estimate (

) then follows from (

) and the assumption (
9.1
), i.e. that
\alpha<\beta\gamma
. The proof of (
9.45
) follows a similar strategy. First, we decompose

and its material derivative as

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle We then decompose
D_{t}\tilde{S}_{i}
as follows:

D_{t}\tilde{S}_{i}=D_{t}\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}+\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}D_{t}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}+\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}D_{t}\mathfrak{D}^{T}\Phiup_{i}\eqcolon I+II+III.

The terms
I
and
III
are estimated in similar ways, so we only estimate
I
. To that end, we note that, using (
9
) and the fact that
f\lesssim 1
, we can obtain that

{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}\leq 1+{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim\ell_{q,i}^{-N}.

Using this bound together with (
9.40
) and (
9.41
), we obtain that

\displaystyle{\left\|I\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}+{\left\|D_{t}\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}+{\left\|D_{t}\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}

\displaystyle{}\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}\ell_{q}^{3\alpha\bb{1}_{N\neq 0}}\ell_{q,i}^{-\alpha}\ell_{q}^{3\alpha\bb{1}_{N\neq 0}}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}.

Combining the above with

\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}^{1+\alpha}=\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q}^{3\alpha}\leq\tau_{q}^{-1},

we see that
I
satisfies (
9.45
).

Coming to
II
, we first note that

{\left\|II\right\|}_{N}\lesssim{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{N}{\left\|D_{t}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{0}{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}+{\left\|\mathfrak{D}\Phiup_{i}\right\|}_{0}^{2}{\left\|D_{t}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}.

Therefore, to prove that
II
satisfies (
9.45
), we need to estimate
D_{t}(\sigma_{i}^{-1}S_{i})
. Recalling (
9.49
), we note that

{\left\|D_{t}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}\leq{\left|f^{\prime}(t)\right|}{\left\|\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\mathaccent 23{S}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle f(t)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma(t)$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle f(t)$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma(t)$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$f(t)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma(t)$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$f(t)$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma(t)$}}$}}{\left\|D_{t}\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N}+\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle f(t)\sigma^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma^{2}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle f(t)\sigma^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma^{2}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$f(t)\sigma^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma^{2}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$f(t)\sigma^{\prime}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma^{2}$}}$}}{\left\|\mathaccent 23{S}\right\|}_{N}\eqcolon P_{1}+P_{2}+P_{3}.

To estimate
P_{1}
, we first note that, by (
9.28
)

f^{\prime}(t)=\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sum_{i}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\partial_{t}\eta_{i}(x,t)\eta_{i}(x,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle|\mathbb{T}^{3}|$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sum_{i}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\partial_{t}\eta_{i}(x,t)\eta_{i}(x,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle|\mathbb{T}^{3}|$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\sum_{i}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\partial_{t}\eta_{i}(x,t)\eta_{i}(x,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|\mathbb{T}^{3}|$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\sum_{i}\int\limits_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\mskip 4.0mu}^{\mskip 4.0mu}\hskip-2.0pt\partial_{t}\eta_{i}(x,t)\eta_{i}(x,t)\mskip 0.0mu\mathrm{d}\mskip 0.5mux$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$|\mathbb{T}^{3}|$}}$}}\lesssim\tau_{q}^{-1}.

Combining this with (
9
), we conclude that

{\left\|P_{1}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}.

To bound
P_{2}
from above, we recall that
f\lesssim 1
, and apply (
9.13
) to conclude that

{\left\|P_{2}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim\Lambdaup\varsigma\ell_{q,i}^{-N}\ell_{q}^{-6\alpha}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}.

We then note that

\varsigma\ell_{q}^{-6\alpha}\delta_{q}^{\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$2$}}$}}}\lambda_{q}\lesssim\tau_{q}^{-1}\lambda_{q+1}^{2\alpha-2\beta\gamma},

so that
P_{2}
also satisfies (
9
).

Coming, finally, to
P_{3}
, we use (
9.9
), (
9
), and (
9
) to conclude that
P_{3}
also satisfies (
9
). Combining the bounds on
P_{1},P_{2},P_{3}
we have just obtained, we conclude that
D_{t}(\sigma_{i}^{-1}S_{i})
also satisfies (
9
), i.e.

{\left\|D_{t}\mathchoice{\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\sigma_{i}$}}}{\scalebox{0.5}[0.5]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}{\scalebox{0.4}[0.4]{$\dfrac{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$S_{i}$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\sigma_{i}$}}$}}\right\|}_{N}\lesssim\tau_{q}^{-1}\ell_{q,i}^{-N}.

Combining this estimate with (
9.40
), we conclude (
9.45
).

To prove (

) and (

), we first prove some estimates on

. Firstly, we note that, thanks to (

)

1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}}{\frac{\raisebox{1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\raisebox{-1.5pt}{$\scriptstyle
